# Copy Inventory

Generated from the React source on 2026-04-30. This document maps user-facing copy to the page or product area where it appears and the job that copy is doing. Dynamic values are represented as {value} where the source uses runtime data.

Scope: src/pages, global layout, published site sections, builder section defaults, and section variants. Test files, build output, CSS/class strings, URLs, and obvious technical strings are excluded.

Total captured copy entries: 5054.

| Clear pricing | Accessibility or media label | src/pages/Trust.tsx:9 |
| The main product offer is a flat $49 payment for two years. Auto-renew is off by default. No fake low entry tier that collapses once the real work starts. | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:10 |
| Beautiful site plus real operations | Accessibility or media label | src/pages/Trust.tsx:13 |
| DayOf is not just a brochure page. The product is meant to carry the wedding website, RSVPs, guest management, review-before-send messaging, seating, travel details, and day-of coordination together, with any still-open proof gaps called out plainly instead of marketed away. | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:14 |
| Guest access handled carefully | Accessibility or media label | src/pages/Trust.tsx:17 |
| {value} Search visibility and guest access are not the same thing, and we should not pretend that means a whole separate unpublished product exists when it does not. | Success/status confirmation | src/pages/Trust.tsx:18 |
| AI helps draft, not secretly operate | Accessibility or media label | src/pages/Trust.tsx:21 |
| AI-backed features can help shape drafts and setup outputs using known wedding data, but couples still review, edit, and decide what gets published or sent. | Success/status confirmation | src/pages/Trust.tsx:22 |
| Core v1 claim | Accessibility or media label | src/pages/Trust.tsx:35 |
| Must ship | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:36 |
| Real product direction | Accessibility or media label | src/pages/Trust.tsx:41 |
| Should ship | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:42 |
| Photo return paths, archive mode, anniversary memory layers, and name-change support can be meaningful, but they should not be used to fake a broader launch claim than the core wedding workflow has earned. | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:44 |
| Not part of the current promise | Accessibility or media label | src/pages/Trust.tsx:47 |
| Cut from promise | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:48 |
| We should not imply external custom domains, advanced analytics, enterprise workflow governance, or magical one-click automation unless those things are actually proven live. | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:50 |
| Trust | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:133 |
| Built to make wedding planning feel calmer, not more manipulative. | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:135 |
| The bar here is simple: say what is real, ship the parts couples actually need, and stop doing the wedding-tech bullshit where basic functionality turns into upsells and surprise gotchas. | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:138 |
| No surprise renewals | Policy/trust/legal copy | src/pages/Trust.tsx:141 |

| What is the dress code? | Question or prompt | src/sections/variants/faq/accordion.tsx:35 |
| Cocktail attire. We ask that guests wear formal or semi-formal attire. Please avoid white, ivory, or cream. | Page explanation or feature copy | src/sections/variants/faq/accordion.tsx:36 |
| Can I bring a plus one? | Question or prompt | src/sections/variants/faq/accordion.tsx:40 |
| Due to limited capacity, we can only accommodate guests who are formally invited. Your invitation will indicate whether a plus one is included. | Page explanation or feature copy | src/sections/variants/faq/accordion.tsx:41 |
| Is there parking at the venue? | Question or prompt | src/sections/variants/faq/accordion.tsx:45 |
| Yes! Valet parking is available at the venue entrance for $40. There are also several parking garages within a short walking distance. | Body/helper copy | src/sections/variants/faq/accordion.tsx:46 |
